"}],["$","span",null,{"style":{"--shiki-light":"#032F62","--shiki-dark":"#9ECBFF"},"children":"\"true on the last firing for this message, false x:leading-7","children":[["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"displayed_text"}]," is cumulative rather than a delta so hook scripts never need to reassemble chunks themselves — each firing carries the full text so far. Firing is debounced (at most every ~200ms) except for the final firing (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final: true"}],"), which always fires once the message ends, so the reply’s tail is never dropped waiting on the debounce window."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Delivery semantics"}]," — what a hook script can rely on:"]}],"\n",["$","ul",null,{"className":"x:[:is(ol,ul)_&]:my-[.75em] x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:list-disc x:ms-[1.5em]","children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Slow hooks see fewer, newer payloads."}]," At most one mid-stream hook execution per message is in flight at a time; while one runs, newer debounced payloads ",["$","em",null,{"children":"replace"}]," the queued one rather than piling up behind it. A hook slower than the debounce window therefore skips intermediate snapshots — lossless, since each payload carries the full cumulative text."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," is never queued behind a stale delivery."]}]," The final payload is dispatched the moment the message ends — alongside a still-running mid-stream execution if there is one (the one exception to the one-at-a-time rule, justified the same way: the final cumulative text strictly supersedes whatever that execution is processing). Your hook always receives the ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," payload, and receives it before the ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"Stop"}]," hook fires. One consequence for stateful hooks: when the final execution overlaps a superseded mid-stream one, their ",["$","em",null,{"children":"completion"}]," order is unspecified — the stale execution may finish after the final one (even after ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"Stop"}],"). Treat ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," as terminal per ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"message_id"}]," and let the cumulative text win, rather than assuming the last execution to finish carries the newest state."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":["The turn waits for ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," delivery to complete — but not forever."]}]," The turn’s end (and the ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"Stop"}]," hook, when it fires) waits up to 5 seconds for the final delivery to finish. A hook that completes within that budget keeps the strongest guarantee: a headless run (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen -p ..."}],") exits only after the hook finished, and the ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," execution completes before ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"Stop"}]," starts. A slower hook still receives ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," first — only the wait for its completion is bounded: in the terminal UI or an ACP session the execution simply finishes in the background, while a headless run exits without waiting. The hook process is not killed on exit; it is left to finish on its own, so a script chaining ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen -p … && next-step"}]," can observe ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"next-step"}]," starting while a slow hook is still running. Hitting this timeout prints a warning on stderr."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Cancellation behaviour depends on timing."}]," A turn cancelled ",["$","em",null,{"children":["before ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," dispatches"]}]," fires no ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," — the message is treated as abandoned, and a consumer that buffers until ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," should treat cancellation-silence as its flush/discard signal (e.g. a timeout fallback). The criterion is the abort signal’s state at the moment the turn ends, not whether every chunk had already streamed — an abort landing in the brief gap before that check can still suppress ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," for a message whose text had, in practice, finished arriving. Cancelling ",["$","em",null,{"children":["after ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}]," has dispatched"]}]," (during the drain wait) is different: the still-running hook execution may be terminated mid-flight (SIGTERM), but the payload itself has already been delivered."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"displayed_text"}]," is provisional until ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final"}],"."]}]," It reflects what has streamed so far; treat intermediate payloads as display state, not as authoritative final content."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"className":"x:my-[.5em]","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"A tool-using turn produces multiple messages."}]," Each model call gets its own ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"message_id"}]," with its own ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"is_final: true"}]," firing: the text before a tool call is one message, the continuation after the tool result is another. Model calls that produce no displayed text (tool-call-only) fire nothing."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Note"}],": Fires in the terminal UI, headless (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"-p"}],"), and ACP (IDE/editor/",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"qwen serve"}],") sessions, with the same payload contract on every
